"chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Advanced reminder engine with localization and intelligent selection.\n\nThis module provides a sophisticated reminder system that:\n- Loads reminders from configurable JSON files\n- Supports multiple languages with fallbacks\n- Implements intelligent reminder selection and deduplication\n- Provides progressive teaching with cooldown periods\n- Uses variable substitution for dynamic content\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderInstance:\n    \"\"\"A single reminder instance with metadata.\"\"\"\n    key: str\n    level: str\n    emoji: str\n    message: str\n    context: Optional[str] = None\n    category: str = \"general\"\n    score: int = 3\n    variables: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    tools_suppressed: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    cooldown_minutes: int = 0\n    last_shown: Optional[datetime] = None\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderContext:\n    \"\"\"Context for reminder generation.\"\"\"\n    tool_name: str\n    project_name: Optional[str]\n    project_root: Optional[str]\n    agent_id: Optional[str]\n    total_entries: int\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float]\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime]\n    docs_status: Dict[str, str]\n    docs_changed: List[str]\n    current_phase: Optional[str]\n    session_age_minutes: Optional[float]\n    variables: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderHistory:\n    \"\"\"Tracks recently shown reminders for deduplication.\"\"\"\n    reminder_hashes: Dict[str, datetime] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    teaching_sessions: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    last_cleanup: datetime = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n\n\nclass ReminderEngine:\n    \"\"\"Advanced reminder engine with localization and intelligent selection.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[str] = None):\n        self.config_path = config_path or \"config/reminder_config.json\"\n        self.reminders_path: Optional[str] = None\n        self.rules_path: Optional[str] = None\n\n        self.config: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.reminders: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.rules: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.variables: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.formatting: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n        self.language = \"en-US\"\n        self.fallback_language = \"en-US\"\n\n        self.history = ReminderHistory()\n        self._cooldown_cache_path: Optional[Path] = None\n        self._cooldown_cache_dirty = False\n\n        self._load_configuration()\n\n    def _load_configuration(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load all configuration files.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            config_file = Path(self.config_path)\n            if config_file.exists():\n                self.config = json.loads(config_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n                self.language = self.config.get(\"language\", \"en-US\")\n                self.fallback_language = self.config.get(\"fallback_language\", \"en-US\")\n\n                base_path = config_file.parent\n                self.reminders_path = base_path / self.config.get(\"reminder_paths\", {}).get(\"templates\", \"reminders\")\n                self.rules_path = base_path / self.config.get(\"reminder_paths\", {}).get(\"rules\", \"reminder_rules.json\")\n\n            self._load_reminders()\n            self._load_rules()\n            self._configure_cooldown_cache()\n            self._load_cooldown_cache()\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Failed to load reminder configuration: {e}\")\n            self._load_fallback_reminders()\n\n    def _load_reminders(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load reminder templates for current language.\"\"\"\n        if not self.reminders_path:\n            return\n\n        # Try to load preferred language\n        lang_file = self.reminders_path / f\"{self.language}.json\"\n        if lang_file.exists():\n            self.reminders = json.loads(lang_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            self.variables = self.reminders.get(\"variables\", {})\n            self.formatting = self.reminders.get(\"formatting\", {})\n            return\n\n        # Fallback to default language\n        fallback_file = self.reminders_path / f\"{self.fallback_language}.json\"\n        if fallback_file.exists():\n            self.reminders = json.loads(fallback_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            self.variables = self.reminders.get(\"variables\", {})\n            self.formatting = self.reminders.get(\"formatting\", {})\n\n    def _load_rules(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load reminder selection rules.\"\"\"\n        if not self.rules_path or not self.rules_path.exists():\n            return\n\n        self.rules = json.loads(self.rules_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    def _load_fallback_reminders(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load minimal fallback reminders.\"\"\"\n        self.reminders = {\n            \"reminders\": {\n                \"logging\": {\n                    \"no_logs_yet\": {\n                        \"level\": \"info\",\n                        \"emoji\": \"\ud83d\udcdd\",\n                        \"template\": \"No progress logs yet. Use append_entry to start the audit trail.\",\n                        \"category\": \"logging\"\n                    }\n                },\n                \"context\": {\n                    \"project_context\": {\n                        \"level\": \"info\",\n                        \"emoji\": \"\ud83c\udfaf\",\n                        \"template\": \"Project: {project_name}\",\n                        \"category\": \"context\"\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        self.config = {\n            \"behavior\": {\"max_reminders_per_call\": 2},\n            \"selection\": {\"priority_order\": [\"urgent\", \"warning\", \"info\"]}\n        }\n\n    def _cleanup_history(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Clean up old reminder history.\"\"\"\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        cleanup_after_hours = self.config.get(\"tracking\", {}).get(\"cleanup_after_hours\", 24)\n        cutoff = now - timedelta(hours=cleanup_after_hours)\n\n        # Remove old reminder hashes\n        self.history.reminder_hashes = {\n            h: t for h, t in self.history.reminder_hashes.items()\n            if t > cutoff\n        }\n\n        # Remove old teaching sessions\n        self.history.teaching_sessions = {\n            k: v for k, v in self.history.teaching_sessions.items()\n            if v > 0  # Sessions reset when count reaches 0\n        }\n\n        self.history.last_cleanup = now\n\n        # Keep the persisted cooldown cache bounded as well.\n        self._cleanup_cooldown_cache()\n        self._save_cooldown_cache()\n\n    def _configure_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\n        override = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_REMINDER_CACHE_PATH\")\n        if override:\n            self._cooldown_cache_path = Path(override).expanduser()\n            return\n\n        behavior = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}\n        path_raw = behavior.get(\"cooldown_cache_path\", \"data/reminder_cooldowns.json\")\n        path_obj = Path(path_raw).expanduser()\n        if not path_obj.is_absolute():\n            path_obj = (settings.project_root / path_obj).resolve()\n        self._cooldown_cache_path = path_obj\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/reminder_engine.py", "byte_size": 23052, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 559, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/reminder_engine.py", "sha256": "952cac2de13c77dc7e729f72d71c740b4ad8e3f608019ab0d3bc76351f25010d"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T11:41:25.522827Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 11:41:25 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 7745, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Router-owned execution context and session identity management.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextvars\nimport os\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n\n\n_CURRENT_CONTEXT: contextvars.ContextVar[\"ExecutionContext   None\"] = contextvars.ContextVar(\n    \"scribe_execution_context\",\n    default=None,\n)\n\n\ndef _utc_now_iso() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass AgentIdentity:\n    agent_kind: str\n    model: Optional[str]\n    instance_id: str\n    sub_id: Optional[str]\n    display_name: Optional[str]\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ExecutionContext:\n    repo_root: str\n    mode: str\n    session_id: str\n    execution_id: str\n    agent_identity: AgentIdentity\n    intent: str\n    timestamp_utc: str\n    affected_dev_projects: list[str]\n    sentinel_day: Optional[str] = None\n    transport_session_id: Optional[str] = None\n    stable_session_id: Optional[str] = None  # NEW - from agent_sessions table\n    bug_id: Optional[str] = None\n    security_id: Optional[str] = None\n    parent_execution_id: Optional[str] = None\n    toolchain: Optional[str] = None\n\n\nclass RouterContextManager:\n    \"\"\"Owns router-generated session/execution identity and current context.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, storage_backend=None) -> None:\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._transport_sessions: Dict[str, str] = {}  # Keep as performance cache\n        self._process_instance_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        self._storage_backend = storage_backend  # NEW: Injected dependency\n\n    async def get_or_create_session_id(self, transport_session_id: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get or create a stable session ID for the given transport session ID.\n\n        Lookup order:\n        1. In-memory cache (fast path)\n        2. Database lookup (persistence layer)\n        3. Create new session and persist\n\n        Args:\n            transport_session_id: Unstable ID from transport layer\n\n        Returns:\n            Stable session UUID that persists across restarts\n        \"\"\"\n        if not transport_session_id:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext requires transport_session_id\")\n\n        async with self._lock:\n            # TIER 1: Check in-memory cache (fast path)\n            existing = self._transport_sessions.get(transport_session_id)\n            if existing:\n                return existing\n\n            # TIER 2: Check database for existing session (persistence layer)\n            if self._storage_backend and hasattr(self._storage_backend, \"get_session_by_transport\"):\n                # NO SILENT ERRORS - let it fail loudly so we can see what's broken\n                db_session = await self._storage_backend.get_session_by_transport(transport_session_id)\n                if db_session and db_session.get(\"session_id\"):\n                    session_id = db_session[\"session_id\"]\n                    # Cache it for future requests (performance optimization)\n                    self._transport_sessions[transport_session_id] = session_id\n                    return session_id\n\n            # TIER 3: Create new session (not found in cache or DB)\n            session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n\n            # Cache immediately\n            self._transport_sessions[transport_session_id] = session_id\n\n            # TIER 3b: Persist to database immediately\n            if self._storage_backend and hasattr(self._storage_backend, \"upsert_session\"):\n                # NO SILENT ERRORS - let it fail loudly so we can see what's broken\n                await self._storage_backend.upsert_session(\n                    session_id=session_id,\n                    transport_session_id=transport_session_id,\n                    repo_root=None,  # Will be set later by set_project\n                    mode=\"sentinel\",  # Default mode\n                )\n\n            return session_id\n\n    def _build_agent_identity(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> AgentIdentity:\n        agent_kind = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_KIND\", \"other\")\n        model = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_MODEL\") or os.environ.get(\"CODEX_MODEL\")\n        sub_id = None\n        display_name = None\n        raw_identity = payload.get(\"agent_identity\")\n        if isinstance(raw_identity, dict):\n            sub_id = raw_identity.get(\"sub_id\") or raw_identity.get(\"sub_id\".lower())\n            display_name = raw_identity.get(\"display_name\")\n        return AgentIdentity(\n            agent_kind=agent_kind,\n            model=model,\n            instance_id=self._process_instance_id,\n            sub_id=sub_id,\n            display_name=display_name,\n        )\n\n    async def build_execution_context(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> ExecutionContext:\n        repo_root = payload.get(\"repo_root\")\n        mode = payload.get(\"mode\")\n        intent = payload.get(\"intent\") or \"\"\n        affected = payload.get(\"affected_dev_projects\") or []\n\n        if not repo_root or not isinstance(repo_root, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext missing required field: repo_root\")\n        if not Path(repo_root).is_absolute():\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext repo_root must be an absolute path\")\n        if mode not in {\"sentinel\", \"project\"}:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext mode must be 'sentinel' or 'project'\")\n        if not intent:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext missing required field: intent\")\n        if not isinstance(affected, list):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext affected_dev_projects must be a list\")\n\n        session_id = payload.get(\"session_id\")\n        if session_id is not None and not isinstance(session_id, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext session_id must be a string\")\n        if not session_id:\n            transport_session_id = payload.get(\"transport_session_id\")\n            if transport_session_id is not None and not isinstance(transport_session_id, str):\n                raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext transport_session_id must be a string\")\n            if not transport_session_id:\n                raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext requires transport_session_id or session_id\")\n            session_id = await self.get_or_create_session_id(transport_session_id)\n        else:\n            transport_session_id = payload.get(\"transport_session_id\")\n        execution_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        timestamp_utc = _utc_now_iso()\n\n        sentinel_day = None\n        if mode == \"sentinel\":\n            sentinel_day = timestamp_utc.split(\"T\", 1)[0]\n\n        agent_identity = self._build_agent_identity(payload)\n\n        return ExecutionContext(\n            repo_root=repo_root,\n            mode=mode,\n            session_id=session_id,\n            execution_id=execution_id,\n            agent_identity=agent_identity,\n            intent=intent,\n            timestamp_utc=timestamp_utc,\n            affected_dev_projects=[str(item) for item in affected],\n            sentinel_day=sentinel_day,\n            transport_session_id=transport_session_id,\n            stable_session_id=payload.get(\"stable_session_id\"),  # NEW - pass through stable session\n            bug_id=payload.get(\"bug_id\"),\n            security_id=payload.get(\"security_id\"),\n            parent_execution_id=payload.get(\"parent_execution_id\"),\n            toolchain=payload.get(\"toolchain\"),\n        )\n\n    def set_current(self, context: ExecutionContext) -> contextvars.Token:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.set(context)\n\n    def reset(self, token: contextvars.Token) -> None:\n        _CURRENT_CONTEXT.reset(token)\n\n    def get_current(self) -> Optional[ExecutionContext]:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.get()\n", "line_end": 194, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"SQLite storage backend (default).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.base import StorageBackend\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.models import (\n    ProjectRecord, DevPlanRecord, PhaseRecord, MilestoneRecord,\n    BenchmarkRecord, ChecklistRecord, PerformanceMetricsRecord,\n    DocumentSectionRecord, CustomTemplateRecord, DocumentChangeRecord, SyncStatusRecord\n)\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import format_utc, utcnow\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.search import message_matches\n\nSQLITE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30\nSQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000\n\n\nclass SQLiteStorage(StorageBackend):\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed persistence with lazy connections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db_path: Path   str) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(db_path).expanduser()\n        self._init_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._write_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._initialised = False\n\n    async def setup(self) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - nothing persistent to close\n        # Connections are short-lived; nothing to do.\n        return None\n\n    async def Filter out suppressed reminders\n        filtered = [r for r in candidates if self._should_show_reminder(r, context)]\n\n        # Sort by priority\n        priority_order = self.config.get(\"selection\", {}).get(\"priority_order\", [])\n        category_weights = self.config.get(\"selection\", {}).get(\"category_weights\", {})\n\n        def get_priority(reminder: ReminderInstance) -> int:\n            # Try priority order first\n            if reminder.key in priority_order:\n                return priority_order.index(reminder.key)\n            # Fall back to category weight\n            return category_weights.get(reminder.level, 999)\n\n        filtered.sort(key=get_priority)\n\n        # for a repo/project (and optionally agent).\"\"\"\n    engine = _get_engine()\n    return engine.reset_cooldowns(project_root=project_root, agent_id=agent_id)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Legacy Compatibility API\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync def get_reminders(\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    tool_name: str,\n    state: Optional[object] = None,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Legacy compatibility wrapper for the original get_reminders function.\n\n    This maintains the exact same interface as the original while using\n    the new reminder engine under the hood.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not project:\n        return []\n\n    # Build the new reminder context from the old format\n    context = await _build_legacy_context(\n        project,\n        tool_name,\n        state,\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        variables=variables,\n    )\n\n    # Use the new engine\n    engine = _get_engine()\n    reminder_instances = await engine.generate_reminders(context)\n\n    # Convert to the old format\n    return engine.to_dict_list(reminder_instances)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Context Building Functions\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync def _build_legacy_context(\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\n    tool_name: str,\n    state: Optional[object],\n    *,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> NewReminderContext:\n    \"\"\"Convert legacy project/state format to new ReminderContext.\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract project information\n    project_name = project.get(\"name\")\n    project_root = project.get(\"root\")\n    log_path = Path(project.get(\"progress_log\", \"\"))\n\n    # Read log information (similar to original _build_context)\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime] = None\n    total_entries = 0\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float] = None\n\n    try:\n        from scribe_mcp.utils.logs import read_all_lines, parse_log_line\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\n\n        for line in lines:\n            if parse_log_line(line):\n                total_entries += 1\n\n        for line in reversed(lines):\n            parsed = parse_log_line(line)\n            if not parsed:\n                continue\n            ts_str = parsed.get(\"ts\")\n            if ts_str:\n                try:\n                    from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n                    last_log_time = parse_utc(ts_str)\n                    delta = utcnow() - last_log_time\n                    minutes_since_log = delta.total_seconds() / 60\n                    break\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n    except Exception:\n        # If we can't read logs, use defaults\n        pass\n\n    # Extract docs information\n    docs_status = {}\n    docs_changed = []\n\n    try:\n        if state:\n            # Try to get docs information from state\n            if hasattr(state, 'projects') and project_name in state.projects:\n                state_project = state.projects[project_name]\n                docs_status = state_project.get(\"docs_status\", {})\n                docs_changed = state_project.get(\"docs_changed\", [])\n\n        # Fall back to checking docs directly\n        if not docs_status and \"docs\" in project:\n            docs = project[\"docs\"] or {}\n            for doc_type, doc_path in docs.items():\n                if doc_type == \"progress_log\":\n                    continue\n                path = Path(doc_path)\n                if not path.exists():\n                    docs_status[doc_type] = \"missing\"\n                else:\n                    try:\n                        content = await asyncio.to_thread(path.read_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n                        # Simple completion check\n                        if \"{{\" in content and \"}}\" in content:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"incomplete\"\n                        elif len(content.strip()) < 400:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"incomplete\"\n                        else:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"complete\"\n                    except Exception:\n                        docs_status[doc_type] = \"missing\"\n\n    except Exception:\n        # If we can't check docs, use empty status\n        pass\n\n    # Get current phase\n    current_phase = None\n    try:\n        phase_plan_path = project.get(\"docs\", {}).get(\"phase_plan\")\n        if phase_plan_path:\n            phase_path = Path(phase_plan_path)\n            if phase_path.exists():\n                import re\n                content = await asyncio.to_thread(phase_path.read_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n                match = re.search(r\"##\\s+Phase\\s+(.+?)\\s*\\(In Progress\\)\", content)\n                if match:\n                    current_phase = match.group(1).strip()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # Get session information\n    session_age_minutes = None\n    try:\n        if state and hasattr(state, 'session_started_at') and state.session_started_at:\n            from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n            start_dt = parse_utc(state.session_started_at)\n            if start_dt:\n                age_delta = utcnow() - start_dt\n                session_age_minutes = age_delta.total_seconds() / 60\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return NewReminderContext(\n        tool_name=tool_name,\n        project_name=project_name,\n        project_root=str(project_root) if project_root else None,\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        total_entries=total_entries,\n        minutes_since_log=minutes_since_log,\n        last_log_time=last_log_time,\n        docs_status=docs_status,\n        docs_changed=docs_changed,\n        current_phase=current_phase,\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {},
